```python
        있던 항목이 "새 자료" 수에 들어가지 않게 한다(ERD 알림함·조회 기록).

        사라진 항목은 지우지 않는다(표에 `removed_at`이 없다). 교수가 항목을 지워도 기록은 남는다.
        """
        row = await self._row(
            "select modules_synced_at from lms_courses"
            " where user_id = %s and canvas_course_id = %s",
            (user_id, canvas_course_id),
        )
        first_load = row is None or row["modules_synced_at"] is None
        before = {
            row["canvas_item_id"]
            for row in await self._rows(
                "select canvas_item_id from lms_module_items where canvas_course_id = %s",
                (canvas_course_id,),
            )
        }
        created: list[SavedItem] = []
        for one in items:
            row = await self._row(
                _UPSERT_ITEM,
                {
                    "canvas": canvas_course_id,
                    "item": one.canvas_item_id,
                    "module": one.module_name,
                    "title": one.title,
                    "kind": one.item_type,
                    "url": one.html_url,
                    "now": now,
                },
            )
            assert row is not None  # insert ... returning은 늘 한 행이다
            if one.canvas_item_id not in before:
                created.append(
                    SavedItem(
                        id=str(row["id"]),
                        module_name=one.module_name,
                        title=one.title,
                    )
                )
        # 첫 수집 표시와 "여기까지 봤다"를 함께 올린다(둘 다 그 사용자 과목 행이다)
        await self.conn.execute(
            "update lms_courses set modules_synced_at = %(now)s,"
            " last_viewed_at = greatest(coalesce(last_viewed_at, %(now)s), %(now)s)"
            " where user_id = %(uid)s and canvas_course_id = %(canvas)s"
            " and modules_synced_at is null",
            {"uid": user_id, "canvas": canvas_course_id, "now": now},
        )
        return ModuleChanges(created=created, first_load=first_load)

    async def assignment_titles(self, user_id: str, ids: list[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
        """과제 id → 캘린더 일정 제목(`[과제] 과목 · 과제`). 사라진 과제·수강 뺀 과목은 빠진다."""
        if not ids:
            return {}
        rows = await self._rows(
            "select a.id::text as id, a.title, c.name as course_name from lms_assignments a"
            " join lms_courses c on c.id = a.lms_course_id and c.user_id = a.user_id"
            " where a.user_id = %(uid)s and a.id = any(%(ids)s::uuid[])"
            "   and a.removed_at is null and coalesce(c.is_active, true)",
            {"uid": user_id, "ids": ids},
        )
        return {
            row["id"]: assignment_title(course_name(row["course_name"]).short_name, row["title"])
            for row in rows
        }

    async def claim_sync(self, user_id: str, *, now: datetime, before: datetime) -> bool:
        """동기화를 시작한다고 한 문장으로 못 박는다. 이미 누가 가져갔으면 False다.

        `last_synced_at`을 **시작할 때** 찍는다. 두 가지를 한꺼번에 막는다 —
        대시보드를 두 탭에서 열어 같은 사용자를 동시에 동기화하는 것(둘째는 False를 받는다),
        그리고 늘 실패하는 사용자가 배치 앞자리를 영구히 차지하는 것(실패해도 시각이 밀린다).
        성공·실패는 `status`·`last_error`로 본다(명세 9장).
        """
        cursor = await self.conn.execute(
            "update lms_connections set last_synced_at = %(now)s"
            " where user_id = %(uid)s and status <> 'disconnected'"
            " and (last_synced_at is null or last_synced_at <= %(before)s)",
            {"uid": user_id, "now": now, "before": before},
        )
        return cursor.rowcount > 0

    async def touch_synced(self, user_id: str, *, now: datetime) -> None:
        """잘 받았다고 적는다. 오류 표시를 지운다.

        시각을 **뒤로 돌리지 않는다** — 느린 동기화가 뒤에 시작한 동기화의 자리(`claim_sync`)를
        지워 버리면 같은 사용자를 또 동기화하게 되고, 그 사이 찍힌 오류 표시도 사라진다.
        해제한 연결은 건드리지 않는다.
        """
        await self.conn.execute(
            "update lms_connections set last_synced_at = %(now)s, status = 'active',"
            " last_error = null"
            " where user_id = %(uid)s and status <> 'disconnected'"
            " and (last_synced_at is null or last_synced_at <= %(now)s)",
            {"uid": user_id, "now": now},
        )

    async def connected_users(self, *, before: datetime, limit: int) -> list[str]:
        """동기화할 사용자(30분 배치). 오래 안 한 사람부터 본다.

        `before` 뒤에 이미 동기화한(또는 동기화를 시작한) 사람은 건너뛴다. 오류 상태도 넣는다 —
        토큰이 되살아나면 거기서 풀린다. 해제한 사람은 토큰이 없어 빠진다. 실패해도 `claim_sync`가
        시각을 밀어 두므로 한 사람이 앞자리를 계속 차지하지 않는다.
        """
        rows = await self._rows(
            "select user_id from lms_connections"
            " where status <> 'disconnected'"
            " and (last_synced_at is null or last_synced_at <= %(before)s)"
            " order by coalesce(last_synced_at, to_timestamp(0)), user_id limit %(limit)s",
            {"before": before, "limit": limit},
        )
        return [str(row["user_id"]) for row in rows]

    async def assignments(
        self, user_id: str, *, state: str = "open", course_id: str | None = None
    ) -> list[AssignmentItem]:
        """과제 목록(명세 9장). LMS에서 사라진 과제는 어느 state에도 넣지 않는다."""
        query = _ASSIGNMENTS + _STATE_FILTERS[state]
        params: dict[str, Any] = {"uid": user_id}
        if course_id is not None:
            query += " and a.lms_course_id = %(course)s"
            params["course"] = course_id
        return by_due([_assignment(row) for row in await self._rows(query, params)])

    async def courses(self, user_id: str, *, now: datetime) -> list[CourseItem]:
        rows = await self._rows(_COURSES, {"uid": user_id, "now": now})
        return ordered([_course(row) for row in rows])

    # ── 조회 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def _rows(self, query: str, params: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await (await self.conn.execute(query, params)).fetchall()

    async def _row(self, query: str, params: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        return await (await self.conn.execute(query, params)).fetchone()


def update_scope(kinds: list[str], course_id: str | None, since: datetime) -> str:
    """피드 조건의 지문. 조건이 다른 목록에는 그 커서를 쓸 수 없다(명세 9장).

    `since`는 UTC로 적는다 — 같은 시각을 다른 오프셋으로 보내도 같은 목록이다. 조건 부분과 `since`를
    `|`로 나눠 두므로, 다음 쪽을 부를 때 커서에서 `since`를 되읽을 수 있다(기본값이 매 요청 달라지는
    문제를 막는다).
    """
    return f"{update_filter(kinds, course_id)}|{since.astimezone(UTC).isoformat()}"


def update_filter(kinds: list[str], course_id: str | None) -> str:
    """지문에서 `since`를 뺀 부분(종류·과목)."""
    return f"{','.join(sorted(kinds))}|{course_id or ''}"


def update_since(scope: str) -> datetime | None:
    """커서 지문에 적힌 `since`. 모양이 틀리면 None이다."""
    try:
        return datetime.fromisoformat(scope.rsplit("|", 1)[1])
    except (IndexError, ValueError):
        return None


def _update(row: dict[str, Any]) -> ModuleItemUpdate | AssignmentUpdate:
    course = AssignmentCourse(
        id=str(row["course_id"]), short_name=course_name(row["course_name"]).short_name
    )
    if row["kind"] == "module_item":
        return ModuleItemUpdate(
            id=str(row["id"]),
            course=course,
            module_name=row["module_name"],
            title=row["title"],
            item_type=row["item_type"],
            html_url=row["html_url"],
            first_seen_at=row["at"].astimezone(KST),
            uploaded_file_id=str(row["uploaded_file_id"]) if row["uploaded_file_id"] else None,
        )
    return AssignmentUpdate(
        id=str(row["id"]),
        course=course,
        title=row["title"],
        due_at=row["due_at"].astimezone(KST) if row["due_at"] else None,
        submission_state=row["submission_state"],
        html_url=row["html_url"],
        first_seen_at=row["at"].astimezone(KST),
    )


def ordered(items: list[CourseItem]) -> list[CourseItem]:
    """화면 이름 순. 같은 이름이면 과목명으로 가른다.

    DB에 `order by`를 맡기지 않는다 — 정렬 규칙(collation)이 환경마다 달라서 한글과 숫자의 앞뒤가
    바뀐다(2026-10-16 CI에서 걸렸다). 파이썬 글자 코드 순으로 정하면 어디서나 같다.
    """
    return sorted(items, key=lambda one: (one.short_name, one.name))


def by_due(items: list[AssignmentItem]) -> list[AssignmentItem]:
    """마감이 가까운 순. 마감이 없는 과제는 뒤로 보낸다.

    `ordered()`와 같은 이유로 DB에 맡기지 않는다(정렬 규칙이 환경마다 다르다).
    """
    return sorted(
        items, key=lambda one: (one.due_at is None, one.due_at or _FAR, one.title, one.id)
    )


def _assignment(row: dict[str, Any]) -> AssignmentItem:
    return AssignmentItem(
        id=str(row["id"]),
        course=AssignmentCourse(
            id=str(row["course_id"]), short_name=course_name(row["course_name"]).short_name
        ),
        title=row["title"],
        due_at=row["due_at"].astimezone(KST) if row["due_at"] else None,
        submission_state=row["submission_state"],
        html_url=row["html_url"],
        planner_task_id=str(row["planner_task_id"]) if row["planner_task_id"] else None,
        calendar_registered=row["calendar_registered"],
    )


def _course(row: dict[str, Any]) -> CourseItem:
    parsed = course_name(row["name"])
    due = row["next_due"]
    return CourseItem(
        id=str(row["id"]),
        canvas_course_id=row["canvas_course_id"],
        name=row["name"],
        short_name=parsed.short_name,
        has_syllabus=row["has_syllabus"],
        portal_syllabus_url=parsed.syllabus_url,
        open_assignments=int(row["open_assignments"]),
        new_materials=int(row["new_materials"]),
        next_due=NextDue(
            kind=due["kind"],
            title=due["title"],
            due_at=datetime.fromisoformat(due["due_at"]).astimezone(KST),
        )
        if due
        else None,
    )


def get_lms_repo(conn: Annotated[AsyncConnection, Depends(get_conn)]) -> LmsRepository:
    return LmsRepository(conn)


LmsRepoDep = Annotated[LmsRepository, Depends(get_lms_repo)]
```

#### `api/app/repositories/runs.py`

```python
"""실행 조회(GET /runs/{id}, API 명세 11장). 실행·단계(tool_calls)와 작업별 결과.

결과는 따로 저장하지 않고 실행이 만든 행에서 다시 읽는다. 준비하기는 그 실행의 계획(prep_plans)·서류·할 일이고,
포스터 추출과 LMS 동기화는 그 실행의 도구 출력(`tool_calls.output`)이다. 동기화 수는 그 실행만의 사실이라
다른 표에서 다시 셀 수 없다(과제 표에는 어느 실행이 넣었는지 적지 않는다).
"""

import logging
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Annotated, Any

from fastapi import Depends
from psycopg import AsyncConnection
from pydantic import ValidationError

from app.db import get_conn
from app.eligibility import KST
from app.schemas.lms import SAVE_TOOL, LmsSyncResult, SyncCounts
from app.schemas.opportunities import DocumentItem, ProcessStep
from app.schemas.posters import (
    EXTRACT_LABEL,
    DuplicateCandidate,
    PosterDraft,
    PosterExtractResult,
)
from app.schemas.posters import (
    EXTRACT_TOOL as POSTER_TOOL,
)
from app.schemas.prepare import PrepareResult, PrepTask, RunError

# 준비하기 단계 이름(화면의 처리 과정). 정해진 순서로 도는 단계라 chosen_by는 pipeline이다.
# 마감일 캘린더 등록은 준비하기 단계가 아니라 따로 부르는 API다(POST /calendar/events, F-32)
PREPARE_LABELS = {
    "get_document_guide": "필요 서류 확인",
    "build_prep_plan": "마감일에서 거꾸로 일정 짜기",
}
# 포스터 추출 단계 이름. Vision 한 번이라 단계도 하나다(app/posters/service.py)
POSTER_LABELS = {POSTER_TOOL: EXTRACT_LABEL}
# LMS 동기화 단계 이름(F-50 연결·F-51 동기화, app/lms/sync.py)
LMS_LABELS = {
    "check_lms_token": "토큰 확인",
    "fetch_lms_courses": "수강 과목 받기",
    "fetch_lms_assignments": "과제·제출 받기",
    "fetch_lms_modules": "주차 자료 받기",
    SAVE_TOOL: "바뀐 것 저장",
}
FAILED_MESSAGE = "준비 일정을 만들지 못했어요. 잠시 후 다시 눌러 주세요."
# 코드를 남기지 못한 실패에 보여 줄 문구. 작업마다 할 말이 다르다(api/CLAUDE.md: 무엇을 하면 되는지)
FAILED_MESSAGES = {
    "lms_sync": "LMS 자료를 가져오지 못했어요. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.",
}
_CODED = re.compile(r"^([A-Z][A-Z_]+): (.+)$", re.S)  # agent_log.error_text의 "코드: 문구"
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunRow:
    id: str
    user_id: str | None
    trigger_type: str
    status: str
    started_at: datetime | None
    finished_at: datetime | None
    latency_ms: int | None
    error_message: str | None

    def error(self) -> RunError | None:
        """학생에게 보여 줄 실패 이유. ApiError로 남긴 것만 그대로 보여 주고 나머지는 내부 기록이다."""
        if self.status != "failed":
            return None
        if self.error_message and (match := _CODED.match(self.error_message)):
            return RunError(code=match.group(1), message=match.group(2))
        message = FAILED_MESSAGES.get(self.trigger_type, FAILED_MESSAGE)
        return RunError(code="INTERNAL_ERROR", message=message)


def _label(tool_name: str) -> str:
    """화면에 보이는 단계 이름. 작업마다 표가 다르고, 표에 없으면 도구 이름 그대로다."""
    return (
        PREPARE_LABELS.get(tool_name)
        or POSTER_LABELS.get(tool_name)
        or LMS_LABELS.get(tool_name)
        or tool_name
    )


class RunRepository:
    def __init__(self, conn: AsyncConnection) -> None:
        self.conn = conn

    async def run(self, run_id: str) -> RunRow | None:
        row = await (
            await self.conn.execute(
                "select id, user_id, trigger_type, status::text as status, started_at, finished_at,"
                " latency_ms, error_message from agent_runs where id = %s",
                (run_id,),
            )
        ).fetchone()
        if row is None:
            return None
        return RunRow(
            id=str(row["id"]),
            user_id=str(row["user_id"]) if row["user_id"] else None,
            trigger_type=row["trigger_type"],
            status=row["status"],
            started_at=_kst(row["started_at"]),
            finished_at=_kst(row["finished_at"]),
            latency_ms=row["latency_ms"],
            error_message=row["error_message"],
        )

    async def steps(self, run_id: str) -> list[ProcessStep]:
        rows = await (
            await self.conn.execute(
                "select seq, tool_name, status::text as status, latency_ms from tool_calls"
                " where run_id = %s order by seq, id",
                (run_id,),
            )
        ).fetchall()
        return [
            ProcessStep(
                seq=row["seq"],
                tool=row["tool_name"],
                label=_label(row["tool_name"]),
                status=row["status"],
                latency_ms=row["latency_ms"],
                chosen_by="pipeline",
                note=None,
            )
            for row in rows
        ]

    async def prepare_result(self, user_id: str, run_id: str) -> PrepareResult | None:
        """그 실행이 만든 내 계획. 없으면(지워짐) None."""
        plan = await (
            await self.conn.execute(
                "select id, opportunity_id from prep_plans where agent_run_id = %s and user_id = %s",
                (run_id, user_id),
            )
        ).fetchone()
        if plan is None:
            return None
        params: dict[str, Any] = {"plan": plan["id"], "oid": plan["opportunity_id"]}
        documents = await (
            await self.conn.execute(
                "select d.id::text as id, d.name, d.issuer, d.how_to, d.lead_days, d.effort_minutes,"
                " d.form_url, coalesce(d.is_required, true) as is_required,"
                " t.id::text as task_id, t.is_done"
                " from opportunity_documents d left join planner_tasks t"
                " on t.prep_plan_id = %(plan)s and t.document_id = d.id"
                " where d.opportunity_id = %(oid)s"
                " order by coalesce(d.is_required, true) desc, d.name, d.id",
                params,
            )
        ).fetchall()
        tasks = await (
            await self.conn.execute(
                "select id::text as id, title, due_date, document_id::text as document_id"
                " from planner_tasks where prep_plan_id = %(plan)s order by sort_order, due_date, id",
                params,
            )
        ).fetchall()
        return PrepareResult(
            prep_plan_id=str(plan["id"]),
            documents=[DocumentItem(**row) for row in documents],
            tasks=[PrepTask(**row) for row in tasks],
            # 마감일 캘린더는 따로 등록한다(POST /calendar/events). 준비하기 결과에는 담지 않는다
            calendar_event=None,
            calendar_error=None,
        )

    async def lms_sync_result(self, user_id: str, run_id: str) -> LmsSyncResult | None:
        """그 동기화가 바꾼 것(명세 8장). 저장 단계가 없으면(F-50 연결 실행 등) None이다."""
        row = await (
            await self.conn.execute(
                "select t.output from tool_calls t join agent_runs r on r.id = t.run_id"
                " where t.run_id = %s and r.user_id = %s and t.tool_name = %s"
                " order by t.seq desc, t.id desc limit 1",
                (run_id, user_id, SAVE_TOOL),
            )
        ).fetchone()
        output = row["output"] if row else None
        if not isinstance(output, dict):
            return None
        try:
            return LmsSyncResult(
                courses=int(output["courses"]),
                assignments=SyncCounts(
                    new=int(output["new"]),
                    due_changed=int(output["due_changed"]),
                    submitted=int(output["submitted"]),
                ),
                # F-52 전 실행에는 이 칸이 없다(그때는 주차 항목을 받지 않았다)
                new_module_items=int(output.get("new_module_items", 0)),
                # 캘린더 자동 등록 전 실행에도 없다
                calendar_events=int(output.get("calendar_events", 0)),
                calendar_moved=int(output.get("calendar_moved", 0)),
            )
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            logger.warning("동기화 결과 모양이 달라 읽지 못했어요: run %s", run_id)
            return None

    async def poster_result(self, user_id: str, run_id: str) -> PosterExtractResult | None:
        """그 실행이 만든 초안. 기록이 지워졌거나 모양이 달라졌으면 None이다."""
        row = await (
            await self.conn.execute(
                "select t.output from tool_calls t join agent_runs r on r.id = t.run_id"
                " where t.run_id = %s and r.user_id = %s and t.tool_name = %s"
                " order by t.seq desc, t.id desc limit 1",
                (run_id, user_id, POSTER_TOOL),
            )
        ).fetchone()
        output = row["output"] if row else None
        if not isinstance(output, dict) or "draft" not in output:
            return None
        try:
            draft = PosterDraft.model_validate(output["draft"])
        except ValidationError:
            logger.warning("포스터 초안 모양이 달라 읽지 못했어요: run %s", run_id)
            return None
        candidate = output.get("duplicate_candidate")
        duplicate = None
        if isinstance(candidate, dict):
            try:
                duplicate = DuplicateCandidate.model_validate(candidate)
            except ValidationError:
                duplicate = None
        return PosterExtractResult(draft=draft, duplicate_candidate=duplicate)


def _kst(value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:
    return value.astimezone(KST) if value else None


def get_run_repo(conn: Annotated[AsyncConnection, Depends(get_conn)]) -> RunRepository:
    return RunRepository(conn)


RunRepoDep = Annotated[RunRepository, Depends(get_run_repo)]
```

#### `api/app/lms/sync.py`

```python
"""LMS 동기화 (F-51, API 명세 8장). 과목 목록 → 과목마다 과제·제출 → 저장 → 플래너·알림.

두 곳에서 부른다. `POST /lms/sync`는 대시보드를 열 때(202 + `run_id`, 결과는 `GET /runs/{id}`)이고,
`POST /internal/jobs/lms-sync`는 30분마다 도는 배치다. 둘 다 같은 `_sync`를 쓴다.

지키는 것:
- 외부 호출은 모두 실행(`lms_sync`) 안의 도구로 기록한다(api/CLAUDE.md). 토큰은 입력에 넣지 않는다
- LearningX를 부르는 동안 요청용 DB 연결을 쥐지 않는다(준비하기와 같다). 받아 둔 뒤 한 트랜잭션에 쓴다
- 과목의 과제 목록을 끝까지 받지 못하면 **그 과목은 저장하지 않는다**. 부분 목록으로 저장하면 못 받은
  과제가 "사라진 과제"가 된다(저장소의 `removed_at`)
- 토큰이 막히면(401) 거기서 멈추고 연결을 `error`로 바꾼다. 화면이 다시 연결을 안내한다
- 마지막 동기화가 5분 안이면 건너뛴다(명세 9장). 대시보드를 여러 번 열어도 LearningX를 또 부르지 않는다

캘린더 등록은 아직 하지 않는다(사용자 구글 캘린더에 쓰는 일이라 따로 정한다). 과목 공지는 F-53이다.

주차 항목(F-52)은 과목 공통이라 저장도 알림도 과목 단위다. 그 과목의 항목을 **이번에 처음** 받으면
한 학기 분량이 모두 "새 항목"이라 알림을 보내지 않고 저장만 한다.
"""

import logging
from collections.abc import Callable
from contextlib import AbstractAsyncContextManager
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Annotated, Any

from fastapi import Depends, Request
from psycopg import AsyncConnection

from app.agent_log import AgentRun, LogConnect, abandon_run, agent_run, start_run
from app.background import RunnerDep, TaskRunner
from app.calendar import EVENT_MINUTES, GoogleCalendarClient, assignment_title, event_id_for
from app.calendar.service import CalendarService
from app.config import Settings, get_settings
from app.crypto import decrypt
from app.eligibility import KST
from app.errors import ApiError
from app.lms.assignments import CanvasAssignment, assignments
from app.lms.client import LearningXClient, LearningXError
from app.lms.modules import CanvasModuleItem, module_items, needs_items
from app.lms.service import API_ERROR, NOT_CONNECTED, TOKEN_EXPIRED, student_courses
from app.repositories.calendar import AssignmentDeadline, CalendarRepository
from app.repositories.lms import (
    AssignmentChanges,
    CourseRow,
    LmsRepository,
    ModuleChanges,
    SavedAssignment,
    SavedItem,
)
from app.repositories.notifications import (
    AssignmentNotice,
    MaterialNotice,
    NotificationRepository,
)
from app.schemas.lms import SAVE_TOOL, SyncCounts, SyncResponse, SyncSkipped, SyncStarted

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
Connect = Callable[[], AbstractAsyncContextManager[AsyncConnection]]

SKIP_AFTER = timedelta(minutes=5)  # 이 안에 동기화했으면 건너뛴다(명세 9장)
BATCH_USERS = 50  # 배치 한 번에 보는 사용자 수 상한. 넘으면 다음 차례에 간다
STOPPED = "동기화를 시작하지 못했어요. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요."
TOKEN_UNREADABLE = "저장한 토큰을 읽을 수 없어요. 다시 연결해 주세요."
NEW_TITLE = "{course} 새 과제가 올라왔어요"
MATERIAL_TITLE = "{course} {module} 자료가 올라왔어요"


class StoredTokenUnreadable(Exception):
    """저장한 토큰을 복호화하지 못했다(암호화 키를 바꿨거나 값이 깨졌다).

    LearningX가 거절한 것이 아니므로 연결을 `error`로 바꾸지 않는다 — 키 문제로 모든 사용자에게
    "토큰이 만료됐어요"를 띄우면 멀쩡한 토큰을 다시 받게 만든다(app/crypto.py의 키 교체).
    """


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Repos:
    """한 트랜잭션에서 쓰는 저장소 묶음. 테스트는 가짜로 바꿔 끼운다(api/CLAUDE.md)."""

    lms: LmsRepository
    notes: NotificationRepository
    calendar: CalendarRepository

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, conn: AsyncConnection) -> "Repos":
        return cls(
            lms=LmsRepository(conn),
            notes=NotificationRepository(conn),
            calendar=CalendarRepository(conn),
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _EventPlan:
    """Google에 넣거나 고칠 일정 하나. 트랜잭션을 닫은 뒤에 쓰려고 미리 모은다."""

    event_id: str
    title: str
    due_at: datetime
    new: bool
    external: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SyncJob:
    run_id: str
    user_id: str
    now: datetime
    token: str = field(repr=False)  # 복호화한 토큰. 실수로 찍지 않게 repr에서 뺀다


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SyncOutcome:
    """동기화 한 번의 결과. `GET /runs/{id}`가 보여 주는 수와 같다(명세 9장)."""

    courses: int = 0
    created: int = 0
    due_changed: int = 0
    submitted: int = 0
    new_items: int = 0  # 처음 본 주차 항목(F-52)
    events: int = 0  # 캘린더에 넣은 과제 마감(F-51)
    moved_events: int = 0  # 마감이 바뀌어 시각을 고친 일정
    notices: int = 0
    failed_courses: int = 0

    def counts(self) -> SyncCounts:
        return SyncCounts(new=self.created, due_changed=self.due_changed, submitted=self.submitted)


@dataclass
class BatchOutcome:
    """배치 한 번의 결과(명세 12장). 실패한 사용자는 다음 차례에 다시 본다."""

    users: int = 0
    synced: int = 0
    failed: int = 0
    skipped: int = 0  # 그새 다른 쪽이 동기화를 시작했거나 연결이 사라진 사람
    new_assignments: int = 0
    due_changed: int = 0
    submitted: int = 0


class LmsSyncService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        transaction: Connect,
        log_connection: LogConnect,
        client: LearningXClient,
        calendar_client: GoogleCalendarClient,
        encryption_key: str,
        runner: TaskRunner,
        repos: Callable[[AsyncConnection], Repos] = Repos.of,
    ) -> None:
        self.transaction = transaction  # 블록이 끝나면 커밋하는 연결(요청 트랜잭션과 따로)
        self.log_connection = log_connection
        self.client = client
        self.calendar_client = calendar_client
        self.key = encryption_key
        self.runner = runner
        self.repos = repos

    # ── 사용자가 눌렀을 때 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def start(self, user_id: str, *, now: datetime) -> SyncResponse:
        """실행 행을 만들고 일을 띄운다. 얼마 전에 동기화했으면 건너뛴 응답(200)이다."""
        async with self.transaction() as conn:
            repo = self.repos(conn).lms
            connection = await repo.connection(user_id)
            if connection is None:
                raise ApiError(404, "LMS_NOT_CONNECTED", NOT_CONNECTED)
            # 한 문장으로 "내가 한다"고 못 박는다. 5분 안에 이미 했거나 다른 탭이 가져가면 False다
            claimed = await repo.claim_sync(user_id, now=now, before=now - SKIP_AFTER)
        if not claimed:
            last = connection.last_synced_at
            return SyncSkipped(last_synced_at=last.astimezone(KST) if last else None)
        try:
            token = self._token(connection.access_token)
        except StoredTokenUnreadable as exc:
            logger.warning("저장한 LMS 토큰을 읽지 못했어요: user %s", user_id)
            raise ApiError(422, "LMS_TOKEN_INVALID", TOKEN_UNREADABLE) from exc
        run_id = await start_run(self.log_connection, trigger="lms_sync", user_id=user_id)
        job = SyncJob(run_id=run_id, user_id=user_id, now=now, token=token)
        work = self.execute(job)
        try:
            await self.runner.start(work)
        except Exception:  # 일을 띄우지 못했다. 실행이 running으로 남지 않게 닫는다
            work.close()
            await self._close(run_id)
            raise ApiError(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR", STOPPED) from None
        return SyncStarted(run_id=run_id, status="running")

    async def execute(self, job: SyncJob) -> None:
        """뒤에서 도는 일. 실패해도 실행 기록에 남고 연결 상태를 바꾼다."""
        try:
            async with agent_run(
                self.log_connection, trigger="lms_sync", user_id=job.user_id, run_id=job.run_id
            ) as run:
                await self._sync(run, job)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("LMS 동기화 실패: user %s, run %s, %r", job.user_id, job.run_id, exc)
            await self._mark_error(job.user_id, exc)
            # 로그 연결을 얻지 못해 agent_run 밖에서 끝난 경우까지 닫는다(준비하기와 같다).
            # 닫지 않으면 GET /runs/{id}가 영원히 running이고 성공률 지표의 분모만 늘어난다
            await self._close(job.run_id)

    # ── 30분 배치 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def sync_all(self, *, now: datetime, limit: int = BATCH_USERS) -> BatchOutcome:
        """연결된 사용자를 차례로 동기화한다. 한 사람이 실패해도 다음 사람을 계속한다."""
        async with self.transaction() as conn:
            users = await self.repos(conn).lms.connected_users(before=now - SKIP_AFTER, limit=limit)
        result = BatchOutcome(users=len(users))
        for user_id in users:
            try:
                outcome = await self._run_for(user_id, now=now)
            except StoredTokenUnreadable:  # 우리 키 문제다. 사용자 연결은 건드리지 않는다
                logger.warning("저장한 LMS 토큰을 읽지 못했어요: user %s", user_id)
                result.failed += 1
                continue
            except Exception as exc:  # 토큰이 막혔거나 LearningX가 답하지 않았다
                logger.warning("LMS 배치 동기화 실패: user %s, %r", user_id, exc)
                result.failed += 1
                await self._mark_error(user_id, exc)
                continue
            if outcome is None:  # 그새 다른 쪽이 동기화를 시작했거나 연결이 사라졌다
                result.skipped += 1
                continue
            result.synced += 1
            result.new_assignments += outcome.created
            result.due_changed += outcome.due_changed
            result.submitted += outcome.submitted
        return result

    async def _run_for(self, user_id: str, *, now: datetime) -> SyncOutcome | None:
        """한 사람을 동기화한다. 그새 다른 쪽이 가져갔거나 연결이 사라졌으면 None이다."""
        async with self.transaction() as conn:
            repo = self.repos(conn).lms
            connection = await repo.connection(user_id)
            if connection is None:  # 그새 해제했다
                return None
            if not await repo.claim_sync(user_id, now=now, before=now - SKIP_AFTER):
                return None
        token = self._token(connection.access_token)
        async with agent_run(self.log_connection, trigger="lms_sync", user_id=user_id) as run:
            return await self._sync(run, SyncJob(run.id, user_id, now, token))

    # ── 동기화 본체 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def _sync(self, run: AgentRun, job: SyncJob) -> SyncOutcome:
        """과목 → 과제 → 저장. 외부 호출을 모두 마친 뒤에 쓰기 트랜잭션을 연다."""
        async with run.tool("fetch_lms_courses") as call:
            courses = student_courses(await self._fetch_courses(job.token))
            call.output = {"courses": len(courses)}
        async with self.transaction() as conn:
            repo = self.repos(conn).lms
            await repo.save_courses(job.user_id, courses, now=job.now)
            rows = await repo.active_courses(job.user_id)
        fetched, failed = await self._fetch_assignments(run, job, rows)
        modules = await self._fetch_modules(run, job, rows)
        if rows and failed and not fetched:
            # 과목이 있는데 한 과목도 받지 못했다. 저장할 것도 없고 성공으로 적으면 안 된다
            # (`touch_synced`가 오류 표시를 지워 버린다). 다음 배치에서 다시 시도한다
            raise ApiError(502, "LMS_API_ERROR", API_ERROR)
        outcome, changes = await self._save(job, fetched, modules, failed=failed)
        outcome = await self._calendar(run, job, outcome, changes)
        async with run.tool(SAVE_TOOL) as call:
            # `GET /runs/{id}`가 이 출력으로 결과를 만든다(repositories/runs.py)
            call.output = {
                "courses": outcome.courses,
                "new": outcome.created,
                "due_changed": outcome.due_changed,
                "submitted": outcome.submitted,
                "new_module_items": outcome.new_items,
                "notices": outcome.notices,
                "failed_courses": outcome.failed_courses,
                "calendar_events": outcome.events,
                "calendar_moved": outcome.moved_events,
            }
        return outcome

    async def _fetch_assignments(
        self, run: AgentRun, job: SyncJob, rows: list[CourseRow]
    ) -> tuple[list[tuple[CourseRow, list[CanvasAssignment]]], int]:
        """과목마다 과제를 받는다. 한 과목이 실패하면 그 과목만 빼고 이어 간다(401은 멈춘다)."""
        fetched: list[tuple[CourseRow, list[CanvasAssignment]]] = []
        failed = 0
        async with run.tool("fetch_lms_assignments", {"courses": len(rows)}) as call:
            for course in rows:
                try:
                    raw = await self.client.assignments(job.token, course.canvas_course_id)
                except LearningXError as exc:
                    failed += self._skip(exc, course, what="과제 목록")
                    continue
                fetched.append((course, assignments(raw)))
            call.output = {
                "courses": len(fetched),
                "assignments": sum(len(items) for _, items in fetched),
                "failed_courses": failed,
            }
        return fetched, failed

    async def _fetch_modules(
        self, run: AgentRun, job: SyncJob, rows: list[CourseRow]
    ) -> list[tuple[CourseRow, list[CanvasModuleItem]]]:
        """과목마다 주차 모듈·항목을 받는다(F-52). 실패한 과목은 그냥 두고 넘어간다.

        과제와 달리 실패를 세지 않는다 — 과제는 받았는데 주차 항목만 못 받았다고 동기화 전체를
        실패로 적을 이유가 없다(새 자료 수가 잠깐 비어 있을 뿐이다).
        """
        fetched: list[tuple[CourseRow, list[CanvasModuleItem]]] = []
        async with run.tool("fetch_lms_modules", {"courses": len(rows)}) as call:
            for course in rows:
                try:
                    modules = await self.client.modules(job.token, course.canvas_course_id)
                    extra = await self._missing_items(job, course, modules)
                except LearningXError as exc:
                    self._skip(exc, course, what="주차 모듈")
                    continue
                fetched.append((course, module_items(modules, extra=extra)))
            call.output = {
                "courses": len(fetched),
                "items": sum(len(items) for _, items in fetched),
            }
        return fetched

    async def _missing_items(
        self, job: SyncJob, course: CourseRow, modules: list[Any]
    ) -> dict[int, list[Any]]:
        """`include[]=items`로 항목이 오지 않은 모듈만 따로 받는다(녹화에서는 없었다)."""
        extra: dict[int, list[Any]] = {}
        for module in modules:
            number = needs_items(module)
            if number is None:
                continue
            extra[number] = await self.client.module_items(
                job.token, course.canvas_course_id, number
            )
        return extra

    def _skip(self, exc: LearningXError, course: CourseRow, *, what: str) -> int:
        """한 과목을 건너뛴다. 토큰이 막힌 경우(401)는 남은 과목도 못 받으니 멈춘다."""
        if exc.unauthorized:
            raise ApiError(422, "LMS_TOKEN_INVALID", TOKEN_EXPIRED) from exc
        logger.warning(
            "%s을 받지 못해 그 과목은 두고 넘어가요: course %s, status %s",
            what,
            course.canvas_course_id,
            exc.status,
        )
        return 1

    async def _save(
        self,
        job: SyncJob,
        fetched: list[tuple[CourseRow, list[CanvasAssignment]]],
        modules: list[tuple[CourseRow, list[CanvasModuleItem]]],
        *,
        failed: int,
    ) -> tuple[SyncOutcome, AssignmentChanges]:
        """받아 둔 과제·주차 항목을 한 트랜잭션에 쓰고, 플래너·알림을 맞춘다.

        바뀐 과제도 함께 돌려준다 — 캘린더 단계(`_calendar`)가 그걸 보고 넣을 것을 고른다.
        """
        changes = AssignmentChanges()
        notices: list[AssignmentNotice] = []
        items = ModuleChanges()
        made = 0
        async with self.transaction() as conn:
            repos = self.repos(conn)
            repo, notes = repos.lms, repos.notes
            for course, assignment_items in fetched:
                one = await repo.save_assignments(
                    job.user_id, course.id, assignment_items, now=job.now
                )
                changes = changes + one
                # 이미 낸 과제는 알리지 않는다(연결 첫 동기화에 지난 과제가 한꺼번에 들어온다)
                notices += [_notice(course, saved) for saved in one.created if not saved.submitted]
            await repo.sync_planner(job.user_id, changes, now=job.now)
            # 제출한 과제는 알림을 멈춘다(명세 10장). 새 과제 알림은 찾은 때 바로 보낸다
            await notes.drop_assignment_notices(job.user_id, changes.submitted)
            made += await notes.add_assignment_notices(job.user_id, notices, now=job.now)
            for course, module_list in modules:
                one_course = await repo.save_module_items(
                    job.user_id, course.canvas_course_id, module_list, now=job.now
                )
                items = items + one_course
                # 주차 항목은 과목 공통이라 그 과목 수강생 모두에게 간다(첫 수집이면 보내지 않는다)
                made += await notes.add_material_notices(
                    course.canvas_course_id,
                    [_material(course, saved) for saved in one_course.notifiable],
                    now=job.now,
                )
            await repo.touch_synced(job.user_id, now=job.now)
        return (
            SyncOutcome(
                courses=len(fetched),
                created=len(changes.created),
                due_changed=len(changes.due_changed),
                submitted=len(changes.submitted),
                new_items=len(items.created),
                notices=made,
                failed_courses=failed,
            ),
            changes,
        )

    # ── 캘린더 (F-51·F-32) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def _calendar(
        self, run: AgentRun, job: SyncJob, outcome: SyncOutcome, changes: AssignmentChanges
    ) -> SyncOutcome:
        """새 과제 마감을 캘린더에 넣고, 바뀐 마감은 이미 넣은 일정의 시각을 고친다.

        설정(`calendar_auto_lms`, 기본 켬)을 끄면 하지 않고, Google 연결이 없으면 조용히 건너뛴다.
        **실패해도 동기화는 성공이다** — 과제·주차 자료는 이미 저장했고, 다음 동기화가 다시 시도한다.
        """
        fresh = [one for one in changes.created if one.due_at and not one.submitted]
        moved = [one for one in changes.due_changed if one.due_at]
        async with self.transaction() as conn:
            repos = self.repos(conn)
            if not await repos.calendar.auto_lms(job.user_id):
                return outcome  # 설정을 껐다. 도구 호출도 열지 않는다
            # 앞 동기화에서 Google이 막혀 넣다 만 과제를 여기서 줍는다(그러지 않으면 영영 안 들어간다)
            done = {one.id for one in fresh} | {one.id for one in moved}
            retry = [
                one
                for one in await repos.calendar.unfinished_assignment_events(job.user_id)
                if one.id not in done
            ]
        if not fresh and not moved and not retry:
            return outcome
        async with run.tool(
            "register_calendar",
            {"new": len(fresh), "due_changed": len(moved), "retry": len(retry)},
        ) as call:
            plan = await self._calendar_plan(job, fresh, moved, retry)
            if plan is None:  # Google 연결이 없거나 토큰을 받지 못했다
                call.output = {"skipped": True}
                return outcome
            token, targets = plan
            made = await self._write_calendar(job, token, targets)
            call.output = {"created": made.events, "moved": made.moved_events}
            return SyncOutcome(
                **{
                    **outcome.__dict__,
                    "events": made.events,
                    "moved_events": made.moved_events,
                }
            )

    async def _calendar_plan(
        self,
        job: SyncJob,
        fresh: list[SavedAssignment],
        moved: list[SavedAssignment],
        retry: list[AssignmentDeadline],
    ) -> tuple[str, list["_EventPlan"]] | None:
        """연결을 보고 넣을 것을 고른다. 자리도 미리 잡아 둔다(일정 번호를 정하려고).

        Google 호출은 이 트랜잭션을 닫은 뒤에 한다(요청용 연결을 쥐고 외부를 부르지 않는다).
        """
        async with self.transaction() as conn:
            repos = self.repos(conn)
            service = self._calendar_service(repos.calendar)
            try:
                token = await service.access_token(job.user_id, job.now)
            except ApiError as exc:
                # 캘린더를 안 쓰는 학생(연결 없음)이거나 Google 토큰 서버가 막혔다.
                # 어느 쪽이든 동기화는 성공이다 — 과제·주차 자료는 이미 저장했다
                logger.info("캘린더를 건너뛰어요: %s", exc.code)
                return None
            titles = await repos.lms.assignment_titles(
                job.user_id, [one.id for one in fresh + moved]
            )
            existing = await repos.calendar.events_for_assignments(
                job.user_id, [one.id for one in moved]
            )
            plans: list[_EventPlan] = []
            for saved in fresh:
                title = titles.get(saved.id)
                if title is None or saved.due_at is None:
                    continue
                row = await repos.calendar.reserve(
                    job.user_id,
                    provider="google",
                    title=title,
                    starts_at=saved.due_at - timedelta(minutes=EVENT_MINUTES),
                    lms_assignment_id=saved.id,
                )
                if row.external_event_id is None:
                    plans.append(_EventPlan(row.id, title, saved.due_at, new=True))
                elif row.starts_at != saved.due_at - timedelta(minutes=EVENT_MINUTES):
                    # 지웠다 다시 올라온 과제다(행은 그대로 살아 있다). 마감이 달라졌으면 고친다
                    plans.append(
                        _EventPlan(
                            row.id, title, saved.due_at, new=False, external=row.external_event_id
                        )
                    )
            for saved in moved:
                row, title = existing.get(saved.id), titles.get(saved.id)
                if row is None or title is None or saved.due_at is None:
                    continue  # 캘린더에 없던 과제는 마감이 바뀌어도 새로 넣지 않는다
                plans.append(
                    _EventPlan(
                        row.id, title, saved.due_at, new=False, external=row.external_event_id
                    )
                )
            for pending in retry:  # 자리만 잡고 못 넣은 과제. 같은 자리로 다시 넣는다
                if pending.due_at is None:
                    continue
                row = await repos.calendar.reserve(
                    job.user_id,
                    provider="google",
                    title=assignment_title(pending.course, pending.title),
                    starts_at=pending.due_at - timedelta(minutes=EVENT_MINUTES),
                    lms_assignment_id=pending.id,
                )
                if row.external_event_id is None:
                    plans.append(_EventPlan(row.id, row.title, pending.due_at, new=True))
        return token, plans

    async def _write_calendar(
        self, job: SyncJob, token: str, plans: list["_EventPlan"]
    ) -> SyncOutcome:
        """Google을 부르고(트랜잭션 밖) 결과를 기록한다. 한 건이 실패해도 나머지를 계속한다."""
        made = SyncOutcome()
        for plan in plans:
            try:
                if plan.new:
                    external = await self._calendar_service(None).insert(
                        token,
                        event_id=event_id_for(plan.event_id),
                        title=plan.title,
                        ends_at=plan.due_at.astimezone(KST),
                    )
                    await self._attach_event(job, plan, external=external)
                    made = SyncOutcome(**{**made.__dict__, "events": made.events + 1})
                    continue
                if plan.external is None:  # 넣다 말았던 일정이다. 다시 넣는다
                    external = await self._calendar_service(None).insert(
                        token,
                        event_id=event_id_for(plan.event_id),
                        title=plan.title,
                        ends_at=plan.due_at.astimezone(KST),
                    )
                    await self._attach_event(job, plan, external=external)
                    made = SyncOutcome(**{**made.__dict__, "events": made.events + 1})
                    continue
                found = await self._calendar_service(None).move(
                    token,
                    event_id=plan.external,
                    title=plan.title,
                    ends_at=plan.due_at.astimezone(KST),
                )
                if found is None:  # 학생이 캘린더에서 지웠다. 되살리지 않는다
                    continue
                await self._attach_event(job, plan, external=plan.external)
                made = SyncOutcome(**{**made.__dict__, "moved_events": made.moved_events + 1})
            except ApiError as exc:
                logger.warning(
                    "과제 마감을 캘린더에 넣지 못했어요: event %s, %s", plan.event_id, exc.code
                )
        return made

    async def _attach_event(self, job: SyncJob, plan: "_EventPlan", *, external: str) -> None:
        async with self.transaction() as conn:
            repos = self.repos(conn)
            await repos.calendar.attach(job.user_id, plan.event_id, external)
            await repos.calendar.move(
                job.user_id,
                plan.event_id,
                starts_at=plan.due_at - timedelta(minutes=EVENT_MINUTES),
            )

    def _calendar_service(self, repo: CalendarRepository | None) -> CalendarService:
        """캘린더 서비스. Google만 부를 때는 저장소가 필요 없다(토큰은 이미 받아 뒀다)."""
        return CalendarService(repo, self.calendar_client, self.key)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    # ── 거들기 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _token(self, encrypted: str) -> str:
        """저장한 토큰을 꺼낸다. 읽지 못하면 StoredTokenUnreadable이다(연결 상태는 건드리지 않는다)."""
        try:
            return decrypt(encrypted, self.key)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise StoredTokenUnreadable from exc

    async def _fetch_courses(self, token: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        try:
            return await self.client.courses(token)
        except LearningXError as exc:
            if exc.unauthorized:
                raise ApiError(422, "LMS_TOKEN_INVALID", TOKEN_EXPIRED) from exc
            logger.warning("LearningX 과목 목록 실패: status %s", exc.status)
            raise ApiError(502, "LMS_API_ERROR", API_ERROR) from exc

    async def _mark_error(self, user_id: str, exc: BaseException) -> None:
        """토큰이 막힌 경우만 연결을 error로 바꾼다. LearningX가 잠깐 답하지 않은 것은 두고 다시 시도한다."""
        if not (isinstance(exc, ApiError) and exc.code == "LMS_TOKEN_INVALID"):
            return
        try:
            async with self.transaction() as conn:
                await self.repos(conn).lms.mark_error(user_id, exc.message)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("연결 상태를 바꾸지 못했어요: user %s", user_id)

    async def _close(self, run_id: str) -> None:
        try:
            await abandon_run(self.log_connection, run_id, ApiError(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR", STOPPED))
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("LMS 동기화 실행을 닫지 못했어요: run %s", run_id)


def _notice(course: CourseRow, saved: SavedAssignment) -> AssignmentNotice:
    """새 과제 알림 문구. 제목은 과목 이름, 본문은 과제와 마감(KST)이다."""
    body = saved.title
    if saved.due_at is not None:
        # %-m은 윈도에서 안 되는 서식이라 손으로 붙인다(테스트·CLI를 윈도에서도 돌린다)
        when = saved.due_at.astimezone(KST)
        body = f"{saved.title} · 마감 {when.month}/{when.day} {when:%H:%M}"
    return AssignmentNotice(
        assignment_id=saved.id, title=NEW_TITLE.format(course=course.short_name), body=body
    )


def _material(course: CourseRow, saved: SavedItem) -> MaterialNotice:
    """새 주차 항목 알림 문구(명세 10장 예시와 같은 꼴): "기계학습 8주차 자료가 올라왔어요"."""
    return MaterialNotice(
        item_id=saved.id,
        title=MATERIAL_TITLE.format(course=course.short_name, module=saved.module_name),
        body=saved.title,
    )


def get_lms_sync_service(
    request: Request,
    runner: RunnerDep,
    settings: Annotated[Settings, Depends(get_settings)],
) -> LmsSyncService:
    state = request.app.state
    pool, log_pool = getattr(state, "pool", None), getattr(state, "log_pool", None)
    if pool is None or log_pool is None:
        raise ApiError(503, "DB_UNAVAILABLE", "데이터베이스에 연결할 수 없어요.")
    return LmsSyncService(
        transaction=pool.connection,
        log_connection=log_pool.connection,
        client=LearningXClient(api_base=settings.lms_api_base),
        calendar_client=GoogleCalendarClient(
            api_base=settings.google_calendar_api_base,
            token_url=settings.google_token_url,
            client_id=settings.google_oauth_client_id,
            client_secret=settings.google_oauth_client_secret,
        ),
        encryption_key=settings.token_encryption_key,
        runner=runner,
    )


LmsSyncServiceDep = Annotated[LmsSyncService, Depends(get_lms_sync_service)]
```

### api (테스트)

#### `api/tests/test_calendar_api.py`

```python
"""마감일 캘린더 등록 API(F-32). Google 호출은 respx로 가로챈다. SQL은 test_calendar_db.py가 본다."""

import json
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any

import httpx
import pytest
import respx
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.clock import current_time
from app.config import Settings, get_settings
from app.crypto import decrypt, encrypt
from app.eligibility import KST
from app.main import create_app
from app.repositories.calendar import (
    AssignmentDeadline,
    GoogleTokens,
    OpportunityDeadline,
    get_calendar_repo,
)
from app.schemas.calendar import CalendarEventView
from tests.conftest import USER_ID, make_token

EVENTS = "/api/v1/calendar/events"
API_BASE = "https://calendar.test/v3"
TOKEN_URL = "https://oauth.test/token"
INSERT = f"{API_BASE}/calendars/primary/events"
ASSIGNMENT_ID = "55555555-5555-4555-8555-555555555555"
OPPORTUNITY_ID = "00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000e1"
EVENT_ID = "00000000-0000-4000-8000-0000000000e2"
GCAL_ID = EVENT_ID.replace("-", "")  # Google 일정 번호는 기록 행의 id에서 만든다
NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 15, 3, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
DEADLINE = datetime(2026, 10, 24, 9, 0, tzinfo=UTC)  # 2026-10-24 18:00 KST


class FakeCalendarRepository:
    def __init__(self, key: str) -> None:
        self.key = key
        self.tokens_row: GoogleTokens | None = GoogleTokens(
            encrypt("access-1", key), encrypt("refresh-1", key), NOW + timedelta(minutes=30)
        )
        self.opportunity: OpportunityDeadline | None = OpportunityDeadline(
            OPPORTUNITY_ID, "한양브레인 장학금", DEADLINE
        )
        self.assignment: AssignmentDeadline | None = AssignmentDeadline(
            ASSIGNMENT_ID, "실습 2주차 결과 제출", "데이터베이스", DEADLINE
        )
        self.events: dict[str, CalendarEventView] = {}
        self.saved: list[tuple[str, int]] = []
        self.added: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.attached: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        self.removed: list[str] = []
        self.uncommitted: list[str] = []  # 아직 확정하지 않은 자리(rollback이 지운다)

    async def tokens(self, user_id: str) -> GoogleTokens | None:
        return self.tokens_row if user_id == USER_ID else None

    async def save_access_token(self, user_id: str, encrypted: str, expires_in: int) -> None:
        self.saved.append((decrypt(encrypted, self.key), expires_in))

    async def prepared_opportunity(
        self, user_id: str, opportunity_id: str
    ) -> OpportunityDeadline | None:
        if user_id != USER_ID or opportunity_id != OPPORTUNITY_ID:
            return None
        return self.opportunity

    async def assignment_deadline(
        self, user_id: str, assignment_id: str
    ) -> AssignmentDeadline | None:
        if user_id != USER_ID or assignment_id != ASSIGNMENT_ID:
            return None
        return self.assignment

    async def event_for(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        provider: str,
        *,
        opportunity_id: str | None = None,
        lms_assignment_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> CalendarEventView | None:
        return self.events.get(f"{opportunity_id or lms_assignment_id}:{provider}")

    async def one(self, user_id: str, event_id: str) -> CalendarEventView | None:
        if user_id != USER_ID:
            return None
        return next((e for e in self.events.values() if e.id == event_id), None)

    async def reserve(self, user_id: str, **values: Any) -> CalendarEventView:
        """실제 저장소처럼 `on conflict ... where external_event_id is null`을 흉내 낸다."""
        target = values.get("opportunity_id") or values.get("lms_assignment_id")
        key = f"{target}:{values['provider']}"
        found = self.events.get(key)
        if found is not None:  # 그새 다른 요청이 잡았거나, 넣다 만 자리가 남아 있다
            if found.external_event_id is not None:
                return found
            fresh = found.model_copy(
                update={"title": values["title"], "starts_at": values["starts_at"]}
            )
            self.events[key] = fresh
            return fresh
        self.added.append(values)
        event = CalendarEventView(
            id=EVENT_ID,
            provider=values["provider"],
            external_event_id=None,  # Google에 넣기 전이다
            title=values["title"],
            starts_at=values["starts_at"],
            all_day=False,
        )
        self.events[key] = event
        self.uncommitted.append(key)
        return event

    async def commit_reservation(self) -> None:
        self.uncommitted.clear()

    def rollback(self) -> None:
        """요청이 예외로 끝나면 커넥션이 하는 일(확정하지 않은 자리는 사라진다)."""
        for key in self.uncommitted:
            self.events.pop(key, None)
        self.uncommitted.clear()

    async def attach(self, user_id: str, event_id: str, external_event_id: str) -> None:
        self.attached.append((event_id, external_event_id))
        for key, event in self.events.items():
            if event.id == event_id:
                self.events[key] = event.model_copy(update={"external_event_id": external_event_id})

    async def remove(self, user_id: str, event_id: str) -> None:
        self.removed.append(event_id)
        for key, event in list(self.events.items()):
            if event.id == event_id:
                del self.events[key]


@pytest.fixture
def calendar(settings: Settings) -> FakeCalendarRepository:
    return FakeCalendarRepository(settings.token_encryption_key)


@pytest.fixture
def client(settings: Settings, calendar: FakeCalendarRepository) -> Iterator[TestClient]:
    tuned = settings.model_copy(
        update={
            "google_calendar_api_base": API_BASE,
            "google_token_url": TOKEN_URL,
            "google_oauth_client_id": "client-1",
            "google_oauth_client_secret": "secret-1",
        }
    )
    app = create_app(tuned)
    app.dependency_overrides[get_settings] = lambda: tuned
    app.dependency_overrides[get_calendar_repo] = lambda: calendar
    app.dependency_overrides[current_time] = lambda: NOW
    with TestClient(app) as test_client:
        yield test_client


@pytest.fixture
def auth() -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {make_token()}"}


def test_calendar_requires_login(client: TestClient) -> None:
    assert client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}).status_code == 401
    assert client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}").status_code == 401


def test_add_event_puts_an_hour_before_the_deadline(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """마감 시각으로 끝나는 1시간짜리를 KST로 넣는다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        route = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert res.status_code == 201
    body = res.json()
    assert body["title"] == "[마감] 한양브레인 장학금"
    assert body["starts_at"] == "2026-10-24T17:00:00+09:00"
    assert (body["external_event_id"], body["all_day"]) == (GCAL_ID, False)
    assert calendar.attached == [(EVENT_ID, GCAL_ID)]
    sent = route.calls[0].request
    assert sent.headers["authorization"] == "Bearer access-1"
    payload = __import__("json").loads(sent.content)
    assert payload["id"] == GCAL_ID  # 일정 번호는 우리가 정한다(다시 넣어도 하나)
    assert payload["summary"] == "[마감] 한양브레인 장학금"
    assert payload["start"] == {"dateTime": "2026-10-24T17:00:00+09:00", "timeZone": "Asia/Seoul"}
    assert payload["end"] == {"dateTime": "2026-10-24T18:00:00+09:00", "timeZone": "Asia/Seoul"}


def test_adding_twice_keeps_one_event(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """다시 눌러도 Google을 또 부르지 않고 200과 그 일정이다(F-32 중복 등록 없음)."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        first = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=False) as router:
        route = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "other"}))
        again = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (first.status_code, again.status_code) == (201, 200)
    assert again.json()["external_event_id"] == GCAL_ID and not route.called
    assert len(calendar.added) == 1


def test_an_expiring_token_is_refreshed_first(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """곧 만료될 토큰은 먼저 갱신해 저장하고, 새 토큰으로 넣는다."""
    calendar.tokens_row = GoogleTokens(
        encrypt("old", calendar.key),
        encrypt("refresh-1", calendar.key),
        NOW + timedelta(seconds=30),
    )
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        token_route = router.post(TOKEN_URL).mock(
            return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"access_token": "access-2", "expires_in": 3599})
        )
        insert = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert res.status_code == 201
    assert insert.calls[0].request.headers["authorization"] == "Bearer access-2"
    assert calendar.saved == [("access-2", 3599)]
    sent = dict(httpx.QueryParams(token_route.calls[0].request.content.decode()))
    assert sent["grant_type"] == "refresh_token" and sent["refresh_token"] == "refresh-1"
    assert sent["client_id"] == "client-1"


def test_no_connection_asks_to_connect(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    calendar.tokens_row = None
    res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "CALENDAR_NOT_CONNECTED")
    assert res.json()["error"]["details"] == {"reason": "not_connected"}
    assert res.json()["error"]["message"] == "Google 캘린더를 연결해 주세요."


def test_a_refused_refresh_asks_to_connect_again(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """Google이 갱신을 거절하면(권한 취소 등) 다시 연결하게 한다."""
    calendar.tokens_row = GoogleTokens(
        encrypt("old", calendar.key), encrypt("refresh-1", calendar.key), NOW - timedelta(minutes=1)
    )
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(TOKEN_URL).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(400, json={"error": "invalid"}))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "CALENDAR_NOT_CONNECTED")
    assert res.json()["error"]["details"] == {"reason": "expired"}


def test_google_errors_become_502(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(500, json={"error": "boom"}))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (502, "GOOGLE_API_ERROR")
    assert "boom" not in res.json()["error"]["message"]  # 바깥 문구를 그대로 내보내지 않는다
    # 자리는 잡아 두되 번호는 붙지 않는다. 다시 누르면 같은 자리를 써서 캘린더에 하나만 들어간다
    assert calendar.attached == []
    calendar.rollback()  # 예외로 끝난 요청의 커넥션이 하는 일. 확정했으면 자리는 남는다
    assert calendar.events[f"{OPPORTUNITY_ID}:google"].external_event_id is None


def test_a_failed_google_call_keeps_the_place_so_the_retry_adds_one_event(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """Google이 502일 때 자리가 사라지면, 다시 눌렀을 때 **다른 번호**로 들어가 일정이 두 개가 된다.

    요청 커넥션은 예외가 나면 통째로 롤백되므로, 자리 잡기는 Google을 부르기 전에 확정해야 한다.
    """
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(500, json={"error": "boom"}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    calendar.rollback()

    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        insert = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "gcal-1"}))
        again = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert again.status_code == 201
    assert len(calendar.added) == 1  # 자리를 새로 만들지 않았다
    assert json.loads(insert.calls[0].request.content)["id"] == EVENT_ID.replace("-", "")


def test_a_place_left_over_from_a_changed_deadline_is_refreshed(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """넣다 만 자리가 남아 있고 그새 마감이 바뀌었으면, 그 자리의 제목·시각을 새로 맞춰 넣는다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(500, json={"error": "boom"}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    moved = DEADLINE + timedelta(days=1)
    calendar.opportunity = OpportunityDeadline(OPPORTUNITY_ID, "한양브레인 장학금 (연장)", moved)

    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        insert = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "gcal-1"}))
        body = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth).json()
    sent = json.loads(insert.calls[0].request.content)
    assert sent["end"]["dateTime"] == moved.astimezone(KST).isoformat()
    assert body["title"] == "[마감] 한양브레인 장학금 (연장)"
    assert body["starts_at"] == (moved - timedelta(hours=1)).astimezone(KST).isoformat()


def test_an_unprepared_or_undated_opportunity_is_refused(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    calendar.opportunity = None
    res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (422, "VALIDATION_FAILED")
    assert res.json()["error"]["details"]["fields"] == {
        "opportunity_id": "준비 일정을 먼저 만들어 주세요."
    }
    calendar.opportunity = OpportunityDeadline(OPPORTUNITY_ID, "상시 공고", None)
    undated = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert undated.status_code == 422
    assert undated.json()["error"]["details"]["fields"]["opportunity_id"].startswith(
        "마감일이 없는"
    )


def test_ics_is_not_ready_yet(client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    res = client.post(
        EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID, "provider": "ics"}, headers=auth
    )
    assert res.status_code == 422
    assert res.json()["error"]["details"]["fields"]["provider"].startswith("지금은 Google")


def test_unknown_fields_are_refused(client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    res = client.post(
        EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID, "syllabus_item_id": "x"}, headers=auth
    )
    assert res.status_code == 422


def test_delete_removes_it_from_google_too(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        route = router.delete(f"{INSERT}/{GCAL_ID}").mock(return_value=httpx.Response(204))
        res = client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}", headers=auth)
    assert res.status_code == 204 and route.called
    assert calendar.removed == [EVENT_ID] and calendar.events == {}


def test_deleting_an_event_google_already_lost_still_works(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """학생이 Google에서 먼저 지웠으면 404가 온다. 우리 기록도 지운다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.delete(f"{INSERT}/{GCAL_ID}").mock(return_value=httpx.Response(404))
        res = client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}", headers=auth)
    assert res.status_code == 204 and calendar.removed == [EVENT_ID]


def test_someone_elses_event_is_not_found(client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    res = client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}", headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (404, "NOT_FOUND")


def test_without_app_credentials_an_expired_token_asks_to_connect(
    settings: Settings, calendar: FakeCalendarRepository, auth: dict[str, str]
) -> None:
    """client_id·secret을 .env에 넣지 않으면 갱신할 수 없다. 다시 연결하게 한다(Google은 부르지 않는다)."""
    bare = settings.model_copy(
        update={"google_calendar_api_base": API_BASE, "google_token_url": TOKEN_URL}
    )
    app = create_app(bare)
    app.dependency_overrides[get_settings] = lambda: bare
    app.dependency_overrides[get_calendar_repo] = lambda: calendar
    app.dependency_overrides[current_time] = lambda: NOW
    calendar.tokens_row = GoogleTokens(
        encrypt("old", calendar.key), encrypt("refresh-1", calendar.key), NOW - timedelta(minutes=1)
    )
    with TestClient(app) as client, respx.mock(assert_all_called=False) as router:
        route = router.post(TOKEN_URL).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={}))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["details"]) == (409, {"reason": "expired"})
    assert not route.called


def test_retrying_after_a_lost_response_keeps_one_event(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """넣는 응답을 못 받고 다시 눌러도 캘린더에 일정이 하나다.

    번호를 우리가 정해 보내므로 두 번째 요청은 같은 번호로 간다. Google은 409(같은 번호가 있다)를
    주는데 그것은 앞 요청이 이미 넣었다는 뜻이라 성공으로 본다.
    """
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(side_effect=httpx.ReadTimeout("늦었다"))
        lost = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert lost.status_code == 502 and calendar.attached == []
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        route = router.post(INSERT).mock(
            return_value=httpx.Response(409, json={"error": "duplicate"})
        )
        again = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert again.status_code == 201
    assert again.json()["external_event_id"] == GCAL_ID
    assert __import__("json").loads(route.calls[0].request.content)["id"] == GCAL_ID
    assert len(calendar.added) == 1  # 자리는 하나만 잡았다


def test_a_quota_error_is_not_a_permission_problem(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]
) -> None:
    """403은 쿼터·한도에도 온다. 다시 연결하라고 하면 학생이 할 수 있는 일이 없다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(
            return_value=httpx.Response(403, json={"error": "rateLimitExceeded"})
        )
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (502, "GOOGLE_API_ERROR")


def test_a_revoked_token_asks_to_connect_again(client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    """401은 권한이 없어진 것이다. 다시 연결하게 한다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(401, json={"error": "invalid"}))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["details"]) == (409, {"reason": "expired"})


def test_a_network_error_is_not_a_permission_problem(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]
) -> None:
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(side_effect=httpx.ConnectError("닿지 못함"))
        res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (502, "GOOGLE_API_ERROR")


def test_a_passed_deadline_is_refused(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """마감이 지난 공고는 준비하기처럼 막는다(지난 일정을 캘린더에 넣지 않는다)."""
    calendar.opportunity = OpportunityDeadline(OPPORTUNITY_ID, "지난 장학", NOW - timedelta(days=1))
    res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (409, "DEADLINE_PASSED")
    assert calendar.added == []


def test_a_bad_opportunity_id_is_a_validation_error(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]
) -> None:
    res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": "공고아님"}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (422, "VALIDATION_FAILED")


def test_deleting_works_even_when_the_permission_is_gone(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """권한이 없어졌으면 Google에서는 못 지운다. 우리 기록은 지워 줄을 없앤다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    calendar.tokens_row = None  # 설정에서 연결을 끊었다
    res = client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}", headers=auth)
    assert res.status_code == 204
    assert calendar.removed == [EVENT_ID] and calendar.events == {}


def test_a_google_outage_keeps_the_record_for_a_retry(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """Google이 잠깐 멈춘 것은 다시 누르게 한다(기록을 지우면 Google 쪽 일정이 고아가 된다)."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.delete(f"{INSERT}/{GCAL_ID}").mock(return_value=httpx.Response(500))
        res = client.delete(f"{EVENTS}/{EVENT_ID}", headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["code"]) == (502, "GOOGLE_API_ERROR")
    assert calendar.removed == []


def test_a_broken_token_asks_to_connect_again(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """암호화 키를 바꿔 복호화하지 못해도 500이 아니라 다시 연결이다."""
    calendar.tokens_row = GoogleTokens("못-푸는-값", "못-푸는-값", NOW + timedelta(minutes=30))
    res = client.post(EVENTS, json={"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (res.status_code, res.json()["error"]["details"]) == (409, {"reason": "expired"})


# ── LMS 과제 마감 (F-51) ────────────────────────────────────────────────────


def test_an_assignment_deadline_goes_in_with_the_course_in_the_title(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    """제목에 과목을 앞세운다 — 캘린더에서 어느 수업 과제인지 바로 보인다."""
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        route = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        response = client.post(EVENTS, json={"lms_assignment_id": ASSIGNMENT_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert response.status_code == 201
    body = response.json()
    assert body["title"] == "[과제] 데이터베이스 · 실습 2주차 결과 제출"
    assert body["external_event_id"] == GCAL_ID
    assert body["all_day"] is False
    sent = json.loads(route.calls[0].request.content)
    assert sent["summary"] == body["title"]
    # 마감 시각으로 끝나는 1시간이다(공고와 같은 규칙)
    assert sent["end"]["dateTime"].startswith(DEADLINE.astimezone(KST).isoformat()[:16])
    assert calendar.added[0]["lms_assignment_id"] == ASSIGNMENT_ID


def test_adding_the_same_assignment_twice_keeps_one_event(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=True) as router:
        router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": GCAL_ID}))
        first = client.post(EVENTS, json={"lms_assignment_id": ASSIGNMENT_ID}, headers=auth)
    with respx.mock(assert_all_called=False) as router:
        route = router.post(INSERT).mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"id": "other"}))
        again = client.post(EVENTS, json={"lms_assignment_id": ASSIGNMENT_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert (first.status_code, again.status_code) == (201, 200)
    assert not route.called
    assert len(calendar.added) == 1


def test_an_assignment_without_a_deadline_is_refused(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    assert calendar.assignment is not None
    calendar.assignment = AssignmentDeadline(ASSIGNMENT_ID, "마감 없는 과제", "데이터베이스", None)
    response = client.post(EVENTS, json={"lms_assignment_id": ASSIGNMENT_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert response.status_code == 422
    assert "lms_assignment_id" in response.json()["error"]["details"]["fields"]


def test_an_assignment_past_its_deadline_is_refused(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str], calendar: FakeCalendarRepository
) -> None:
    calendar.assignment = AssignmentDeadline(
        ASSIGNMENT_ID, "지난 과제", "데이터베이스", NOW - timedelta(days=1)
    )
    response = client.post(EVENTS, json={"lms_assignment_id": ASSIGNMENT_ID}, headers=auth)
    assert response.status_code == 409
    assert response.json()["error"]["code"] == "DEADLINE_PASSED"


def test_an_assignment_that_is_not_mine_is_not_found(
    client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]
) -> None:
    other = "66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666666"
    response = client.post(EVENTS, json={"lms_assignment_id": other}, headers=auth)
    assert response.status_code == 404
    assert response.json()["error"]["code"] == "NOT_FOUND"


def test_exactly_one_target_is_required(client: TestClient, auth: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    both = {"opportunity_id": OPPORTUNITY_ID, "lms_assignment_id": ASSIGNMENT_ID}
    assert client.post(EVENTS, json=both, headers=auth).status_code == 422
    assert client.post(EVENTS, json={}, headers=auth).status_code == 422
    assert client.post(EVENTS, json={"provider": "google"}, headers=auth).status_code == 422
    # 강의계획서 일정은 F-14와 함께 연다(아직 모르는 칸이다)
    syllabus = {"syllabus_item_id": "77777777-7777-4777-8777-777777777777"}
    assert client.post(EVENTS, json=syllabus, headers=auth).status_code == 422
```

#### `api/tests/test_calendar_db.py`

```python
"""캘린더 기록 SQL(F-32·F-51)을 실제 스키마에 대고 확인한다: 토큰, 준비한 공고, LMS 과제 마감,
등록·중복·시각 고치기·취소·격리.

트랜잭션을 되돌려 지운다. DATABASE_URL이 있을 때만 돈다.
"""

from datetime import timedelta
from typing import Any

import pytest

from app.eligibility import KST
from app.repositories.calendar import CalendarRepository
from app.repositories.opportunities import OpportunityRepository
from tests.db_world import DATABASE_URL, World, connect, make_user

pytestmark = [
    pytest.mark.db,
    pytest.mark.anyio,
    pytest.mark.skipif(not DATABASE_URL, reason="DATABASE_URL이 없어서 DB 통합 테스트를 건너뜀"),
]


@pytest.fixture
async def calendar() -> Any:
    """준비를 마친 공고 하나와 준비하지 않은 공고 하나, 사용자 둘."""
    conn = await connect()
    try:
        world = await World.start(conn)
        prepared = await world.add("준비한 장학", days=9)
        bare = await world.add("준비 안 한 장학", days=9)
        user = await make_user(conn, grade=3)
        other = await make_user(conn, grade=3)
        run = await (
            await conn.execute(
                "insert into agent_runs (user_id, trigger_type, status)"
                " values (%s, 'prepare', 'succeeded') returning id",
                (user,),
            )
        ).fetchone()
        await conn.execute(
            "insert into prep_plans (user_id, opportunity_id, agent_run_id) values (%s, %s, %s)",
            (user, prepared, run["id"]),
        )
        yield CalendarRepository(conn), user, other, prepared, bare, world.now
    finally:
        await conn.rollback()
        await conn.close()


async def test_only_a_prepared_opportunity_is_offered(calendar: Any) -> None:
    repo, user, other, prepared, bare, now = calendar
    found = await repo.prepared_opportunity(user, prepared)
    assert found is not None and found.title == "준비한 장학"
    assert found.deadline_at is not None
    assert await repo.prepared_opportunity(user, bare) is None  # 준비하지 않은 공고
    assert await repo.prepared_opportunity(other, prepared) is None  # 남의 계획


async def test_a_running_plan_is_not_prepared_yet(calendar: Any) -> None:
    """상세·플래너와 같은 기준(PREP_DONE)이다. 실행 중인 계획은 캘린더에도 넣지 못한다."""
    repo, user, _, prepared, _, _ = calendar
    await repo.conn.execute(
        "update agent_runs set status = 'running' where id ="
        " (select agent_run_id from prep_plans where user_id = %s and opportunity_id = %s)",
        (user, prepared),
    )
    assert await repo.prepared_opportunity(user, prepared) is None


async def test_reserve_is_one_per_opportunity(calendar: Any) -> None:
    """자리는 공고마다 하나다. 두 번째 요청은 같은 행을 돌려받아 같은 Google 번호를 쓴다."""
    repo, user, _, prepared, _, now = calendar
    ends_at = (now + timedelta(days=9)).astimezone(KST)
    event = await repo.reserve(
        user,
        provider="google",
        opportunity_id=prepared,
        title="[마감] 준비한 장학",
        starts_at=ends_at - timedelta(hours=1),
    )
    assert (event.provider, event.external_event_id, event.all_day) == ("google", None, False)
    again = await repo.reserve(
        user,
        provider="google",
        opportunity_id=prepared,
        title="[마감] 준비한 장학",
        starts_at=ends_at - timedelta(hours=1),
    )
    assert again.id == event.id
    await repo.attach(user, event.id, "gcal-1")
    done = await repo.one(user, event.id)
    assert done is not None and done.external_event_id == "gcal-1"
    rows = await (
        await repo.conn.execute(
            "select count(*) as n from calendar_events where user_id = %s", (user,)
        )
    ).fetchone()
    assert rows["n"] == 1


async def test_find_and_remove(calendar: Any) -> None:
    repo, user, other, prepared, _, now = calendar
    ends_at = (now + timedelta(days=9)).astimezone(KST)
    event = await repo.reserve(
        user,
        provider="google",
        opportunity_id=prepared,
        title="[마감] 준비한 장학",
        starts_at=ends_at - timedelta(hours=1),
    )
    await repo.attach(user, event.id, "gcal-1")
    assert (await repo.event_for(user, "google", opportunity_id=prepared)).id == event.id
    assert await repo.event_for(user, "ics", opportunity_id=prepared) is None
    assert await repo.one(other, event.id) is None  # 남의 일정은 안 보인다
    await repo.remove(other, event.id)  # 남의 일정은 못 지운다
    assert await repo.one(user, event.id) is not None
    await repo.remove(user, event.id)
    assert await repo.one(user, event.id) is None


async def test_tokens_are_read_and_the_access_token_is_replaced(calendar: Any) -> None:
    repo, user, _, _, _, _ = calendar
    assert await repo.tokens(user) is None  # 연결 전
    await repo.conn.execute(
        "insert into oauth_tokens (user_id, provider, access_token, refresh_token, expires_at)"
        " values (%s, 'google', 'enc-access', 'enc-refresh', now() + interval '55 minutes')",
        (user,),
    )
    tokens = await repo.tokens(user)
    assert tokens is not None
    assert (tokens.access_token, tokens.refresh_token) == ("enc-access", "enc-refresh")
    await repo.save_access_token(user, "enc-new", 3599)
    after = await repo.tokens(user)
    assert after is not None
    assert after.access_token == "enc-new"
    assert after.refresh_token == "enc-refresh"  # refresh token은 그대로 둔다
    assert after.expires_at is not None and not after.expired(after.expires_at - timedelta(hours=1))


async def test_a_reserved_event_is_not_shown_as_registered(calendar: Any) -> None:
    """Google에 넣기 전의 자리는 공고 상세에서 '등록됨'으로 보이지 않는다(F-32).

    보이면 학생이 "캘린더에서 빼기"만 누를 수 있고, 실제로는 캘린더에 아무것도 없다.
    """
    repo, user, _, prepared, _, now = calendar
    ends_at = (now + timedelta(days=9)).astimezone(KST)
    event = await repo.reserve(
        user,
        provider="google",
        opportunity_id=prepared,
        title="[마감] 준비한 장학",
        starts_at=ends_at - timedelta(hours=1),
    )
    detail = await OpportunityRepository(repo.conn).detail(
        user, prepared, now=now, display_name="테스트 학생"
    )
    assert detail is not None and detail.prep.calendar_events == []
    await repo.attach(user, event.id, "gcal-1")
    after = await OpportunityRepository(repo.conn).detail(
        user, prepared, now=now, display_name="테스트 학생"
    )
    assert after is not None
    assert [ref.id for ref in after.prep.calendar_events] == [event.id]


# ── LMS 과제 마감 (F-51) ────────────────────────────────────────────────────


async def _assignment(
    conn: Any, user: str, *, due: Any, title: str = "실습 2주차 결과 제출"
) -> str:
    """과목 하나와 과제 하나를 넣고 과제 id를 돌려준다."""
    course = await (
        await conn.execute(
            "insert into lms_courses (user_id, canvas_course_id, name)"
            " values (%s, 214023, '202620HY23525_데이터베이스') returning id",
            (user,),
        )
    ).fetchone()
    row = await (
        await conn.execute(
            "insert into lms_assignments (user_id, lms_course_id, canvas_assignment_id, title,"
            " due_at, submission_state) values (%s, %s, 11, %s, %s, 'unsubmitted') returning id",
```
